# Research record (452 sources, 203 high priority, 1 verified)

## Infrastructure and lifeline status feeds for North Carolina during a disaster (electric, telecom, roads/bridges, water/wastewater, fuel, health, shelters, 911, airports, rail, schools, mail)
- (high/high) **Duke Energy Distribution Outages (public ArcGIS FeatureServer)** (Duke Energy) https://services3.arcgis.com/oX5r75R7mapdoI2F/ArcGIS/rest/services/Outages/FeatureServer
  Hosted feature service 'Duke Energy Distribution Outages for public use' with 3 layers: 0 Outage Points by Affected Customers (fields jurisdiction, start_time, cause, crew_status, affected_customers), 1 Outages by County and Customers Out (polygons; county_name, state_name, customer_served, customer_out, customer_with_power, percent_out, etr), 2 Outage Affected Areas (polygons). Service text: 'Data are updated approximately every 20 minutes'.
  access: ArcGIS REST FeatureServer (query, JSON, maxRecordCount 2000, WGS84) | auth: none | licence: Copyright text 'Duke Energy'; no explicit licence, described as 'for public use' | cadence: ~20 minutes | NC: Duke Energy Carolinas and Duke Energy Progress service territory (most of NC except co-op, municipal and Dominion areas); extent -89.3 to -74.0 lon covers Carolinas, Midwest and Florida jurisdictions
  use (during,post): Primary electric lifeline overlay on the Conditions map (county percent_out choropleth + outage points with cause/crew status); county customer_out as a need-index term and as a displacement multiplier (no power x damage); corroborating evidence for road-isolation E8 (clusters of outages along a corridor + closures); calibration target for the nighttime-lights outage proxy; poll every 20 min and archive snapshots because the service exposes no history.
  caveats: No ETR on point layer; 'customer' = meter not person; ArcGIS Online hosted service could be withdrawn without notice; no stated terms of reuse, so confirm with Duke Energy before public redistribution.
- (medium/medium) **North Carolina's Electric Cooperatives Live Outage Map** (North Carolina Electric Membership Corporation / NC Electric Cooperatives (site built by Global Reach)) https://outages.ncelectriccooperatives.com/outages/maps
  Statewide Leaflet web map of co-op outages with 'Details by County' and 'Details by Cooperative' (members affected, highest impact) and a weather layer. Cooperatives page states 'Data is refreshed every 15 minutes.' The map bundle calls same-origin endpoints /outages/county/info, /outages/cooperative/info, /outages/details, /{build}/outages/maps/counties.geojson and /local/modules/electriccooperatives/assets/maps/cooperatives.geojson via jQuery ajax.
  access: Web map only; undocumented internal JSON endpoints (bare GET returns 'Not Found' page, calling convention unknown) | auth: none for the map; no API offered | licence: '© 2026 North Carolina's Electric Cooperatives. All rights reserved'; no data terms | cadence: 15 minutes | NC: All NC electric cooperative territories (search snippet: 26 co-ops serving ~2.5 M people in 93 of 100 counties; not confirmed on primary page). Covers most of the Helene-hit western counties where Duke is not the distributor.
  use (during,post): County outage counts for co-op territory to complete the statewide electric lifeline picture (Duke + co-ops + Dominion + municipals); need-index and displacement terms for rural counties. Requires a data-sharing agreement with NCEMC before any automated pull; alternative is to request NCEM's aggregated feed (see ReadyNC entry).
  caveats: Scraping undocumented endpoints without permission is fragile and may breach site terms; cooperatives geojson gives territory polygons useful even if counts are unavailable.
- (low/medium) **Dominion Energy Outage Map (Kubra Storm Center)** (Dominion Energy (platform: KUBRA)) https://outagemap.dominionenergy.com/
  Outage map for Dominion's VA/NC/SC electric customers. Page source loads https://kubra.io/product/5.54.1/static/js/main.js, confirming the KUBRA Storm Center platform; data endpoints are KUBRA's undocumented JSON services.
  access: Web map; undocumented KUBRA JSON endpoints | auth: none for the map | licence: unstated / proprietary | cadence: unknown (likely 15 min) | NC: Dominion Energy North Carolina: northeastern NC counties only
  use (during,post): Fill the NE-NC gap in the electric lifeline overlay for coastal/Albemarle events; county outage counts as need-index term. Needs Dominion or NCEM agreement; otherwise rely on NCEM aggregate or PowerOutage.us.
  caveats: Vendor confirmed, endpoint schema not documented; refresh interval not confirmed (a secondary site says ~15 min).
- (low/low) **ElectriCities of NC Outage Map (municipal public power)** (ElectriCities of North Carolina) https://www.electricities.com/services/emergency-assistance/outage-map/
  Outage map under ElectriCities' Emergency Assistance program covering NC public power (municipal) utilities; site states 'more than 70' NC municipal utilities and ~100 member communities in NC/SC/VA. Platform and cadence not disclosed on the fetched pages.
  access: Web map only | auth: none | licence: unstated | cadence: unknown | NC: NC municipal electric utilities (e.g., Wilson, Rocky Mount, High Point, New Bern, Greenville) statewide
  use (during,post): Complete electric coverage for municipal utility towns in the lifeline overlay; manual check until a feed is negotiated via NCEM.
  caveats: Map technology, granularity and data access unknown; page content is JS-driven.
- (high/high) **ReadyNC Power Outages (NC Emergency Management aggregate)** (NC Department of Public Safety / NC Emergency Management (ReadyNC)) https://www.readync.gov/power-outages
  State page listing Duke Energy, Dominion Energy, NC Electric Cooperatives, ElectriCities and 26 individual co-ops/municipals with map links and phone numbers. States: 'Power outage data from these utilities is automated and updates approximately every 30 minutes.' No download or API on the page.
  access: Web page with links; the underlying 30-minute automated aggregation is internal to NCEM (request-only) | auth: request-only for the aggregated feed | licence: unstated (NC state government) | cadence: ~30 minutes (per page text) | NC: Statewide, all distribution utilities
  use (during,post): Procurement target: ask NCEM for read access to its automated statewide outage aggregation (county x utility customers out every 30 min) rather than scraping four vendor maps; would feed the lifeline overlay, need index and displacement estimate uniformly.
  caveats: Public page exposes no data; feed existence inferred from the page's own statement about automated 30-minute updates.
- (medium/medium) **PowerOutage.us / PowerOutage.com Live Outage API** (Bluefire Studios LLC (PowerOutage.us)) https://poweroutage.com/products
  Commercial aggregation of utility outage maps: Live Outage REST API 'updated every 10 minutes' with 'GeoJSON outage shapes directly from utility providers'; embeddable map; text/email alerts; CSV reports; historical data to city level at 10-minute intervals 'partially back to 2016'. Claims tracking of 94% of US electric customers. Government agencies are listed as users (poweroutage.us/about/utility).
  access: REST API (JSON/GeoJSON), CSV via email/FTP/SFTP, embeddable map | auth: paid subscription; contact sales for pricing | licence: commercial / proprietary | cadence: 10 minutes | NC: All major NC utilities aggregated (Duke, Dominion, co-ops, municipals) at utility/state/county/city level
  use (pre,during,post): Single-vendor fallback for a complete NC electric outage time series (county + utility + GeoJSON shapes) if utility/NCEM agreements are not in place; historical back-fill for validating the nighttime-lights proxy. Flag: paid; pricing not published.
  caveats: poweroutage.us/products returned HTTP 403 to this fetch; details taken from the sister poweroutage.com products page. No government/non-profit pricing tier stated. Juvare resells it as an add-on.
- (high/medium) **EAGLE-I (Environment for Analysis of Geo-Located Energy Information) - live platform** (US DOE Office of Cybersecurity, Energy Security, and Emergency Response (CESER), operated by Oak Ridge National Laboratory) https://eagle-i.doe.gov/
  DOE's near-real-time energy situational-awareness GIS covering electric outages (county level, 15-minute, >92% of US customers), petroleum and natural gas. NASEO/NARUC FAQ: 'Access to this tool is limited to DOE-approved accounts. State Energy Officials and Public Utility Commission staff can request an account.' DOE's State Energy Security Action List tells states to 'Sign-up for EAGLE-I account' for 'Real-time situational awareness allows you to monitor and report outages'. Account request: https://eagle-i.doe.gov/login/accountRequest.
  access: Web platform (login); API availability to account holders unknown | auth: DOE-approved account (state energy officials, PUC staff, emergency managers involved in energy emergency response); not public | licence: US Government system; redistribution terms unknown | cadence: 15 minutes (per ORNL description of collection) | NC: Statewide county-level outage counts for all NC utilities with public maps
  use (pre,during,post): The authoritative free source of statewide county outage counts for a state EM user, but only via an NCEM/NC Energy Office account; NC Recon should support manual CSV import or an operator-side connector run inside NCEM's enclave rather than embedding EAGLE-I credentials. Procurement: NCEM to request accounts and ask DOE/ORNL about machine access and redistribution limits.
  caveats: eagle-i.doe.gov landing page is a JS shell (no public documentation); whether county EM offices (not just state) qualify is not stated; API terms unknown.
- (medium/high) **EAGLE-I Power Outage Data 2014-2022 (public historical dataset; figshare v3 extends to 2023)** (Oak Ridge National Laboratory (DOE)) https://doi.ccs.ornl.gov/dataset/ccec86f0-e144-5de8-aee0-fb26028b26e1
  County-level, 15-minute electricity outage records (FIPS, county, state, customers out, timestamp) 'collected from utility's public outage maps using an ETL process', plus state-level coverage metrics per year. DOI 10.13139/ORNLNCCS/1975202. A figshare release (DOI 24237376, v3 2025-04-10) extends coverage to 2023 per search metadata. PNNL's derived 'Event-correlated Outage Dataset in America' on OpenEI (2014-2023, CC BY 4.0) joins it with DOE-417 and county population.
  access: Portal download (ORNL DOI page via Globus; figshare; OpenEI zip 29.8 MB) | auth: none (Globus may need a free account) | licence: ORNL page: not stated; OpenEI derivative: Creative Commons Attribution 4.0; figshare page not fetchable (403) | cadence: static (annual releases) | NC: All NC counties 2014-2023 at 15-minute resolution (coverage share varies by year)
  use (pre,post): Back-testing: pair historical county outage curves (Matthew 2016, Florence 2018, Fred 2021) with VNP46A2 radiance drops to fit and validate the nighttime-lights outage proxy before deployment; baseline restoration-curve priors for the displacement model. Hurricane Helene (Sept 2024) is NOT in the 2014-2023 release.
  caveats: 'the number of customers does not necessarily equate to the number of people affected'; no 2024+ data; licence on the ORNL page unstated.
- (low/medium) **DOE-417 / OE-417 Electric Emergency Incident and Disturbance Reports** (US DOE (CESER; online system hosted by PNNL; annual tables on oe.netl.doe.gov)) https://www.oe.netl.doe.gov/OE417_annual_summary.aspx
  Mandatory utility filings of major grid disturbances. Form instructions: thresholds include '50,000 or more customers' affected and loss of '300 MW or more'; filing timelines 1 hour (emergency), 6 hours, and 72-hour final; Schedule 1 is public, Schedule 2 confidential. Annual summary tables (Excel/PDF) of events since 2000 with date, area affected, NERC region, event type, demand loss, customers affected.
  access: Portal download (Excel/PDF annual tables) | auth: none | licence: US Government public (not explicitly stated) | cadence: per event filing; annual public tables | NC: Events listing NC/Duke/Dominion areas; coarse (utility/region text, not county)
  use (pre,post): Historical event catalogue to seed the events planner (plan/events.py) with past NC grid emergencies and to label EAGLE-I history; not a live feed.
  caveats: oe.netl.doe.gov was unreachable from this environment (IPv6 connection refused / DNS), so the download page itself was not verified in this session; description of annual tables is from search metadata and the PNNL instructions PDF.
- (high/high) **NASA Black Marble VNP46A2 (and VJ146A2) daily nighttime lights** (NASA GSFC / LAADS DAAC) https://ladsweb.modaps.eosdis.nasa.gov/missions-and-measurements/products/VNP46A2
  'VIIRS/NPP Gap-Filled Lunar BRDF-Adjusted Nighttime Lights Daily L3 Global 15 arc-second Linear Lat Lon Grid' (~500 m), Collection 2 (ArchiveSet 5200), HDF5 ~40 MB/tile, 2012-present; DOI 10.5067/VIIRS/VNP46A2.002. Black Marble project page: VIIRS DNB 750 m source, products 'within a three-hour window' near-real-time, products also from NOAA-20 (VJ146) and NOAA-21 in development; distribution via LAADS DAAC, Earthdata Search, Worldview; Earthdata Login required; NASA open data policy.
  access: LAADS DAAC archive and API (token), Earthdata Search, Worldview | auth: free Earthdata Login / LAADS token | licence: NASA open data (US Government, free and open) | cadence: daily; NRT within ~3 hours per project page | NC: Global daily; NC covered by tiles h10v05/h11v05-ish (15 arc-sec grid); every clear night
  use (pre,during,post): Nighttime-lights outage proxy: compute per-tract and per-county radiance ratio of post-event nights to a pre-event VNP46A3 monthly baseline, mask clouds/snow via QA, produce an 'estimated share of lights out' layer where utility feeds are missing (co-op/municipal gaps) and as an independent evidence channel for displacement; also a coverage-gap and 'dark community' detector for field-team tasking.
  caveats: Cloud cover after landfall often blocks the first nights; 500 m is tract-scale not structure-scale; NRT 3-hour claim is from the project page, VNP46A2 product page does not state latency; Black Marble HD (30 m) is a research product, no public NC Helene HD product was confirmed (NASA Disasters portal pages were JS shells/404 here).
- (medium/medium) **EOG VIIRS Nighttime Light nightly mosaics** (Earth Observation Group, Payne Institute, Colorado School of Mines) https://eogdata.mines.edu/products/vnl/
  Nightly DNB mosaics at 15 arc-second resolution 'updated in near real-time as new imagery arrives' using later-on-top mosaicking, plus monthly/annual composites. Site: 'Many of the VIIRS Nighttime Lights data are available under Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International license'; cite EOG and relevant papers.
  access: Portal download (HTTPS); account/registration option exists | auth: free EOG account likely required for downloads (not confirmed for nightly) | licence: CC BY 4.0 for many products (quoted); check per product | cadence: nightly, NRT | NC: Global nightly tiles covering NC
  use (during,post): Alternative/redundant nightly radiance source for the outage proxy when LAADS is delayed; CC BY licence simplifies redistribution of derived outage maps.
  caveats: Nightly mosaics are not BRDF/lunar corrected like VNP46A2; registration terms not confirmed.
- (high/high) **FCC Disaster Information Reporting System (DIRS) Communications Status Reports** (Federal Communications Commission, Public Safety and Homeland Security Bureau) https://docs.fcc.gov/public/attachments/DOC-406055A1.pdf
  Daily PDF 'Communications Status Report for Areas Impacted by Hurricane Helene' during a DIRS activation. Contents verified from the Oct 1, 2024 report: county list in the activation area (21 NC counties); 911 section naming each PSAP 'rerouted without ALI' / 'rerouted with ALI' / 'operational without ALI' (NC: Yancey rerouted without ALI; Beech Mountain PD, Cherokee, Clay, EBCI Primary, Madison, Mitchell, Swain rerouted with ALI); wireless table per county with Cell Sites Served, Cell Sites Out, Percent Out, Out Due to Damage, Out Due to Transport, Out Due to Power, Up on Back-up Power (NC total 1452 served, 707 out, 48.69%); cable/wireline subscribers out aggregated for the whole disaster area (796,999); TV/FM/AM stations out per state.
  access: Portal download: PDFs posted on fcc.gov/docs.fcc.gov per activation day; no API | auth: none | licence: US Government public domain | cadence: daily during activation (~9:00 a.m. EDT snapshot) | NC: Only counties inside the activation area (Helene: Ashe, Avery, Buncombe, Burke, Caldwell, Cherokee, Clay, Graham, Haywood, Henderson, Jackson, Macon, Madison, McDowell, Mitchell, Polk, Rutherford, Swain, Transylvania, Watauga, Yancey); reports ran Sept 27 - Oct 19, 2024
  use (during,post): Telecom lifeline overlay by county (percent cell sites out, cause split) and PSAP status badges on the Conditions map; 'out due to transport' as corroborating evidence for road/fiber-corridor isolation E8; county percent-out as a need-index term (communications-denied population); PSAP rerouting as a trigger to prioritise those counties for field reporting. Requires a PDF table parser and a watcher for new DOC-*.pdf links.
  caveats: fcc.gov HTML pages returned HTTP 403 to this fetch (docs.fcc.gov PDFs work); only published while DIRS is activated; county-level only, no site locations; provider-level data is confidential; report is a 'snapshot in time'.
- (high/high) **NCDOT TIMS Statewide Incident Web Services (traffic-prod v1)** (NC Department of Transportation, Traffic Systems Operations Unit) https://tims.ncdot.gov/tims/V2/webservices
  REST endpoints: /incidents, /incidents/{id}, /events, /events/{id}/incidents, /regions, /regions/{id}/incidents|weather|alert, /counties, /counties/{id}/incidents|conditions|alert, /roads, /roads/{id}/incidents|weather, base https://eapps.ncdot.gov/services/traffic-prod/v1/. Parameters verbose, recent, active, activeOnDate. TIMS is populated in real time by NCDOT staff and partners in all 100 counties (crashes, closures, work zones, weather events).
  access: REST API (JSON) | auth: none stated on the web services page | licence: unstated on page | cadence: real time as entered | NC: Statewide, all 100 counties, state-maintained roads
  use (pre,during,post): Already used for DriveNC closures; extend to /counties/{id}/conditions and /events/{id}/incidents to scope an event, and use Road Condition = 'Road Closed' on primary routes as the backbone of road-isolation evidence E8 (graph cut on NCDOT routes). Archive incident snapshots for post-event analysis.
  caveats: Rate limits, formats and terms are not documented on the page; a public ArcGIS feature layer of TIMS incidents (used by NCDOT's GO!NC dashboard) exists but its REST URL was not located (metadata PDF 404).
- (high/high) **DriveNC Developer API (incl. WZDx)** (NCDOT) https://www.drivenc.gov/developers/doc
  REST API at https://www.drivenc.gov/api/ for Snow & Ice conditions, Cameras, Message Signs, Rest Areas, Visitor/Welcome Centers, Events, Special Alerts, Ferries, Weigh Stations, plus a WZDx feed at https://www.drivenc.gov/api/wzdx. 'A registered account is needed before you can sign up for a Developer API key'; 'The query string parameter key is required for most calls'; 'Throttling is enabled. Ten calls every 60 seconds.'
  access: REST API | auth: free developer key (account registration) | licence: unstated | cadence: near real time | NC: Statewide
  use (pre,during,post): Cameras (visual ground truth for flooding/road status near a town), ferries (coastal lifeline), Special Alerts and Events as triggers; WZDx closures as a standard interchange format for E8 and exports. Respect 10 calls/60 s with a cache.
  caveats: Throttle is tight for many counties; licence for redistribution unstated.
- (high/high) **NCDOT Bridges & Other Structures (NCDOT_Structures MapServer and quarterly FGDB)** (NCDOT GIS Unit / Bridge Maintenance Unit) https://gis11.services.ncdot.gov/arcgis/rest/services/NCDOT_Structures/MapServer
  'Point file representing North Carolina bridges and other structures, extracted and attributed by the NCDOT Bridge Maintenance Unit's bridge database.' Layers: 0 Bridge Structures, 1 Pipes, 2 Culverts, 3 Railroad Bridge Structures, 4 Tunnel Structures, 5 Pedestrian Structures, 6 Signs, 7 Other Structures. Latest publication 03/15/2026. NCDOT GIS Data Layers page offers quarterly FGDB downloads (May, Aug, Nov, Feb) incl. 'NCDOT Bridges & Other Structures' and Rail Division data.
  access: ArcGIS REST MapServer (query JSON/geoJSON/PBF, max 2000) and portal FGDB download | auth: none | licence: Copyright 'NCDOT GIS Unit'; no licence text | cadence: quarterly | NC: Statewide, all state-maintained structures
  use (pre,during,post): Bridge/culvert nodes in the road-isolation graph for E8: a closure or modelled flood depth at a structure cuts the edge; pre-event layer for the planner; join with NBI condition/scour attributes.
  caveats: Spatial reference 2264 (NC State Plane feet) needs reprojection; fields not enumerated in this fetch.
- (medium/high) **NCDOT Post-Helene Structure Inspection Status (AGOL feature layer)** (NCDOT Structures Management Unit (GO!NC ArcGIS Online org NuWFvHYDMVmmxMeM)) https://services.arcgis.com/NuWFvHYDMVmmxMeM/ArcGIS/rest/services/2024_NCDOT_SMU_Post_Helene_Structure_Inspection_Status/FeatureServer/0
  Point layer of structures in Helene counties with Struct_No, Division, County, STRUCT_SYS, Route, Status (Inspected - No Damage, Inspection Scheduled, Likely Repair, Likely Replace, No Inspection Scheduled), Struct_Type, Damage_Type, Detour_Length, Has_Outlet, Over_Waterway_, Insp_Link, Google_Link; last updated Dec 16, 2024.
  access: ArcGIS REST FeatureServer (JSON, max 2000) | auth: none | licence: unstated (NCDOT) | cadence: static since Dec 2024 | NC: Western NC Helene-affected divisions, 2024
  use (post): Ground truth for bridge/culvert damage in Helene back-tests; template schema for ingesting NCDOT's live inspection status in the next event (ask SMU for the live layer); Detour_Length directly feeds isolation cost in E8.
  caveats: Event-specific layer; may be removed; not a standing feed.
- (medium/medium) **NCDOT BridgeWatch alerts (AGOL BridgewatchAllAlerts / BridgeWatchCountyLevelAlerts)** (NCDOT (GO!NC AGOL org)) https://services.arcgis.com/NuWFvHYDMVmmxMeM/ArcGIS/rest/services/BridgewatchAllAlerts/FeatureServer/0
  Point layer 'all_alerts' with fields stream_nam, devicesGag, structure_, road, corridor, county, storm, peakValue, value_, probabilit, river_basi, nexradMeas, ncb_scour_, ncb_suff_r, ncb_adt, ncb_drift, ncb_fractu, status, advNumber, advTimeEST, timeTriEST, receded, yrPkNew, data_time; county service described as 'Bridge Watch County Level Alerts DEV'. Field names indicate gauge/NEXRAD-driven bridge scour and overtopping alerts per structure and storm.
  access: ArcGIS REST FeatureServer (JSON, max 1000) | auth: none | licence: unstated | cadence: unknown | NC: Statewide extent (-84.3 to -75.5 lon)
  use (during,post): Potential trigger layer: bridges with active scour/overtopping alerts become probable cuts in E8 and inspection priorities; confirm with NCDOT Hydraulics whether a production (non-DEV) service exists.
  caveats: Labelled DEV; last edit July 21, 2023; purpose not documented on the service; may be a stale prototype.
- (medium/high) **FHWA National Bridge Inventory (NBI) ASCII files** (Federal Highway Administration) https://www.fhwa.dot.gov/bridge/nbi/ascii.cfm
  Annual per-state bridge inventory with Coding Guide items (condition ratings, scour critical, ADT, detour length, coordinates). Years 1992-2025; '2025 was the last year data was submitted to FHWA in the Coding Guide's ASCII format' (transition to SNBI); 'published on this website by June 15th of each calendar year'.
  access: Portal download (per-state ASCII, delimited) | auth: none | licence: US Government public domain (not explicitly stated) | cadence: annual (June) | NC: All NC public highway bridges
  use (pre,post): Enrich NCDOT structures with condition and scour-critical codes to weight bridge-failure likelihood in E8 and in the pre-event vulnerability view; detour length for isolation cost.
  caveats: Format changing to SNBI after 2025; NC file not individually verified in this fetch.
- (medium/medium) **Waze for Cities data partnership (via NCDOT's existing membership)** (Waze (Google)) https://web-assets.waze.com/partners/ccp/Waze-for-Cities-v01.pdf
  'The Waze for Cities Data program is a free, two-way data exchange' with 'nearly 1,000 partners including city, state, and country government agencies, academic institutions, and first responders.' Partners receive system-generated traffic jams (slow-downs vs. time-of-day average from anonymized GPS) and user-reported incidents 'Jams, crashes, hazards, construction, potholes, roadkill, stopped vehicles, objects on road, and missing signs'; partners provide closures/events. NCDOT already shares TIMS closures to Waze through an automated 'CIFS v2 data feed' and participates in the two-way program.
  access: Partner feed (JSON/GeoRSS per secondary sources) under agreement | auth: partnership agreement (free); government agencies eligible | licence: proprietary; redistribution/public display terms governed by agreement (not public) | cadence: near real time (secondary: ~2 minutes) | NC: Statewide wherever Waze users drive
  use (during,post): Crowd corroboration of impassable roads and detours for E8 (alerts of type ROAD_CLOSED/HAZARD near a modelled cut); obtain through NCDOT's or NCEM's existing Waze for Cities account rather than a new agreement; keep Waze-derived layers internal unless the agreement allows display.
  caveats: Feed format and 2-minute cadence come from secondary sources; sparse user density in rural western NC after cell outages.
- (medium/medium) **NCDOT GO!NC ArcGIS Online hurricane-support layers (Airport_Status_With_Link, Ferry_Terminals, Camstations_A, Helene_HWM_NCGS, Helene_Cell_Location_Planning_Map, Asheville_Crowdsourced_Disaster)** (NCDOT (ArcGIS Online org NuWFvHYDMVmmxMeM)) https://services.arcgis.com/NuWFvHYDMVmmxMeM/ArcGIS/rest/services?f=pjson

  access: ArcGIS REST FeatureServers (JSON/geoJSON/PBF) | auth: none | licence: unstated (NCDOT) | cadence: event-driven | NC: Statewide / event-specific (Helene 2024, Florence 2018)
  use (during,post): Shows NCDOT publishes event lifeline layers on AGOL during storms; build a generic 'AGOL layer import' so analysts can add these as overlays on day one; airport runway status schema is a template for an airports lifeline layer.
  caveats: Ad hoc, event-specific, often stale (airport layer last edited 2018); no SLA.
- (medium/medium) **EPA SDWIS via Envirofacts Data Service API** (US EPA) https://www.epa.gov/enviro/envirofacts-data-service-api

  use (pre,post): Water lifeline inventory: population served per system and counties served as denominators for 'people on systems without power/under advisory'; pre-event vulnerability (surface-water systems near flood zones). Not a live status source.

- (medium/high) **NC OneMap NC1Map_Water_Sources (public water supply intakes and wells)** (NC DEQ Division of Water Resources, Public Water Supply Section with NC CGIA) https://services.nconemap.gov/secure/rest/services/NC1Map_Water_Sources/MapServer

  caveats: EPSG 32119; no treatment plant or distribution boundaries.

  Section page links Drinking Water Watch (state SDWIS portal), 'PWS Reports (Sampling Status Report)' at pws.ncwater.org, Local Water Supply Plans, and county contacts; phone (919) 707-9100. No public list of active boil-water advisories or system operational status was found on the fetched pages.
  access: Web portals; status data request-only | auth: none for portals; status feed would need DEQ agreement | licence: unstated (NC state) | cadence: unknown | NC: All NC public water systems
  use (during,post): Procurement: ask DEQ PWS for the water/wastewater system status spreadsheet they maintain during declared events (Helene) as a CSV import; until then, boil-water status is a manual field-report attribute in NC Recon.
  caveats: Drinking Water Watch (pwss.enr.state.nc.us/NCDWW2) returned HTTP 403 to this fetch; deq.nc.gov/helene and /hurricane-helene returned 404; no wastewater (WWTP/SSO) status feed located.
- (low/medium) **GasBuddy Fuel Availability Tracker** (GasBuddy (PDI Technologies)) https://tracker.gasbuddy.com/
  Crowd-sourced station-level map activated during hurricanes showing four states: has fuel and power, no power, limited fuel, no fuel or power; version 4.3.0 (2026). Press coverage reports metro-level 'percent of stations without gasoline' figures during Irma/Ida; GasBuddy notes outage figures exclude stations without power.
  access: Web map only; no public API | auth: none for the map | licence: proprietary | cadence: continuous crowd updates when activated | NC: NC stations when the tracker is activated for the region
  use (during,post): Manual situational awareness link in the Conditions panel; automated ingestion would need a GasBuddy data agreement.
  caveats: Data source and update cadence not stated on the page; crowd reports thin where cell service is down.
- (low/high) **EIA Open Data API v2** (US Energy Information Administration) https://www.eia.gov/opendata/
  Free API (registration key) with petroleum weekly supply, hourly electric grid monitor by balancing authority (demand, forecast, net generation, interchange), and retail prices. 'EIA data is provided free of charge'; bulk updates 5 a.m. and 3 p.m. ET. Weekly retail gasoline price series cover only CA, CO, FL, MA, MN, NY, OH, TX, WA and 10 cities - North Carolina is not included; EIA publishes no fuel availability/outage data.
  access: REST API (JSON) and bulk files | auth: free API key | licence: EIA Copyrights and Reuse Policy (US Gov, free reuse) + API Terms of Service | cadence: hourly (grid monitor), weekly (petroleum) | NC: Hourly demand for Duke Energy Carolinas (DUK) and Duke Energy Progress (CPLE/CPLW) balancing authorities; PADD 1C regional fuel
  use (during,post): Coarse state-scale electricity-demand drop as a sanity check on statewide outage magnitude; regional fuel supply context for the brief. Low value for town-scale triage.
  caveats: No county detail; no NC gasoline price series.
- (high/high) **HHS emPOWER REST Service (Medicare electricity-dependent beneficiaries)** (HHS Administration for Strategic Preparedness and Response (ASPR) emPOWER Program) https://empowerprogram.hhs.gov/empower-rest.html
  Public ArcGIS feature service (https://services2.arcgis.com/ZQ4jTQn6k7VPXEwO/arcgis/rest/services/HHS_emPOWER_REST_Service_Public/FeatureServer) with monthly updated de-identified totals of Medicare FFS and Advantage beneficiaries with claims for 14 types of electricity-dependent DME/cardiac devices (ventilators, oxygen concentrators, home dialysis, etc.) at national, state, county and ZIP levels, plus total Medicare enrolment; 'publicly available' and intended to be 'consumed and displayed as a data layer in other GIS'.
  access: ArcGIS REST FeatureServer (JSON, max 2000) | auth: none | licence: US Government public (terms not stated) | cadence: monthly | NC: All NC counties and ZIP codes
  use (pre,during,post): Need-index term: electricity-dependent Medicare population x county/ZIP outage share (Duke/co-op/nighttime lights) to rank where prolonged outages are life-threatening; pre-event planning layer; brief statistic for county EM. Individual-level emPOWER Emergency Response data is separate and restricted to public health authorities on request.
  caveats: Small-count suppression rules not described on the page; Medicare only (excludes under-65 non-Medicare).
- (medium/low) **NC SMARTT (State Medical Asset Resource Tracking Tool)** (NC DHHS Office of Emergency Medical Services / Healthcare Preparedness Program) https://www.ncdhhs.gov/divisions/health-service-regulation/office-emergency-medical-services
  State-required web tool in which all NC hospitals, health centres and EMS systems report open/available beds and resources daily (per published research using SMARTT: 108 acute care hospitals in 75 counties). Access limited to authorised healthcare/EM users; no public API found.
  access: request-only (NC DHHS OEMS/HPP account) | auth: MOU / authorised user | licence: restricted state data | cadence: daily (reporting requirement) | NC: Statewide hospitals and EMS
  use (during,post): Hospital lifeline overlay (status, bed availability, diversion) for the responder view only, via NCEM/OEMS data-sharing agreement; drives 'nearest open hospital' distance in the need index.
  caveats: NC DHHS pages for SMARTT returned 404 in this session; details rest on peer-reviewed secondary sources.
- (low/high) **HHS 'COVID-19 Reported Patient Impact and Hospital Capacity by Facility' (discontinued)** (US HHS (HealthData.gov)) https://healthdata.gov/Hospital/COVID-19-Reported-Patient-Impact-and-Hospital-Capa/anag-cw7u
  Weekly facility-level hospital utilisation (129 columns) for all CMS-registered hospitals; Public Domain U.S. Government; last updated May 3, 2024 and 'will no longer be updated because hospitals are no longer required to report'.
  access: Socrata portal / API | auth: none | licence: Public Domain U.S. Government | cadence: static | NC: All NC hospitals through May 2024
  use (pre): Historical bed-capacity baseline per hospital for the pre-event vulnerability view; no live value. Confirms there is currently no public facility-level bed status feed.
  caveats: Discontinued.
- (low/low) **KCER / ESRD Network dialysis facility status** (CMS Kidney Community Emergency Response (KCER) contractor and ESRD Networks (NC = Network 6)) https://esrdncc.org/en/kcer/
  ESRD Networks 'Collect open/closed/altered treatment status from facilities' and 'Provide to KCER an Emergency Situation Status Report (ESSR) for all facilities in impacted area' during emergencies (IPRO ESRD Network deck). Secondary sources point to dialysisunits.com for nearest-open-facility maps ('Open' = potable water, electricity, staff, supplies and performing dialysis). kcercoalition.com now redirects to an IPRO resource archive.

- (medium/high) **NC OneMap NC1Map_Health (hospitals, medical facilities, nursing homes)** (NC State Center for Health Statistics / Division of Facility Services via NC CGIA) https://services.nconemap.gov/secure/rest/services/NC1Map_Health/MapServer
  Layers 0 Hospitals, 2 Medical Facilities, 3 Nursing Homes (no dialysis or pharmacies). Update cadence not stated.
  access: ArcGIS REST MapServer/FeatureServer (JSON/geoJSON/PBF) | auth: none | licence: NC CGIA (public state data) | cadence: unknown | NC: Statewide

'The FEMA ESF6-SS database is synchronized every morning with the American Red Cross shelter database' and FEMA GIS then checks for updates every 20 minutes. Contact FEMA-GISMAPS@fema.gov.

  use (during,post): Shelter overlay with population and capacity; reconcile estimated displaced persons against sheltered population per county (displaced minus sheltered = unmet need); public tract summary can list open shelters; distribution channel for the brief.
  caveats: Only shelters entered by partners; county-run shelters may lag; capacity fields often null.
- (medium/medium) **ReadyNC Open Shelters (NC Emergency Management)** (NC Department of Public Safety / NCEM) https://www.readync.gov/stay-informed/open-shelters
  State list page 'Emergency shelters are open at these locations:' populated during events; note that corporate donations must go through the NC Business EOC. Data source and cadence not stated; no map or API.
  access: Web page (list) | auth: none | licence: unstated (NC state) | cadence: event-driven manual updates | NC: Statewide state/county shelters

- (medium/high) **NC OneMap NC1Map_Emergency_Services and NG911 NC911_Regions (PSAP boundaries, EOCs, EMS stations)** (NC CGIA / NC 911 Board) https://services.nconemap.gov/secure/rest/services/NC1Map_Emergency_Services/MapServer

'This API sits on the legacy side of the FAA's NOTAM modernization'; the replacement NMS-API requires contacting notams@faa.gov.

Amtrak alerts page lists passenger and station advisories by route (e.g., 'Piedmont and Carolinian Service Adjustments Effective August 31 - October 8, 2026') with no feed/API.

  use (pre,post): Static rail network for lifeline context (rail bridges as structures; washouts as damage evidence from imagery); no live status feed - note Helene washed out NS and CSX lines east/west of Asheville, which only carrier releases reported.

  Page of mail service disruptions by region with a 'Last update' timestamp (e.g., 'Oct. 6, 2026, 9:30 p.m. ET'), split into residential, business mailer and international sections; business section offers a downloadable 'Mail Service Disruption Report' spreadsheet; no RSS or API.
  access: Web page + spreadsheet download | auth: none | licence: USPS (US Government corporation); unstated | cadence: frequent during events | NC: NC entries appear only when disruptions occur (none at fetch time)
  use (post): Low-priority recovery indicator (post offices closed / delivery suspended) for the brief; manual or scheduled scrape of the spreadsheet.
  caveats: Regional free text, not geocoded.
- (medium/medium) **HIFLD Open (Homeland Infrastructure Foundation-Level Data) lifeline layers** (DHS Geospatial Management Office) https://hifld-geoplatform.hub.arcgis.com/
  Historically ~320 public critical-infrastructure datasets (hospitals, nursing homes, fire/EMS stations, PSAP boundaries, cellular towers, substations, transmission lines, electric retail service territories, power plants, pharmacies, shelters) as CSV/KML/SHP/FGDB and GeoService/GeoJSON APIs. Status in 2025-2026 is uncertain: the hub landing page is a JS shell, its DCAT feed returned HTTP 500, DHS's HIFLD page describes only HIFLD Secure (GII account, Login.gov, Data Use Agreement, FOUO/commercial data), and the Data Rescue Project archived 'HIFLD OPEN Fire and EMS Stations' on Aug 26, 2025 (DataLumos project 239161). A NASA NCCS mirror (maps.nccs.nasa.gov/mapping/rest/services/hifld_open/...) exists but timed out.
